### Key architectural framing locked through this point

#### The B vs D vs C three-way analysis (last response synthesis; NOT yet persisted to §18)

The user's "B vs D" question surfaced that **the actual choice is three-way: B vs C vs D**.

**B (mega-cell, hash-keyed)**:
- Fastest reads + simplest architecture + lowest memory
- ✗ Whole-cell wake on every env write (poor residuation precision for mutual recursion)
- ✗ No per-name structural first-class-ness

**C (mega-cell + component-paths per name)**:
- Read cost = B (audit confirmed)
- ✓ Per-name wake precision via mechanism (component-paths)
- ✗ No per-name structural first-class-ness (still hash-keyed at the lookup layer)

**D (registry cell + per-name binding cells)**:
- Slower reads (2× cell-read overhead, +135-193% vs A; +88-168% vs B)
- ✓ Per-name wake precision via structure
- ✓ Per-name as first-class network entity (each name has cell-id)
- ✓ Phase 11b diagnostic chains anchor per-name cell-id
- ✓ Per-name worldview retraction cleaner (cell-clear vs hasheq-filter)
- ✓ LSP per-name queries route directly
- ✓ `.pnet` per-name serialization first-class
- ✓ PReduce e-class alignment (per-class cells = per-name cells pattern)
- ✓ First-Class by Default principle

**D's extra cost buys**: structural per-name first-class-ness for downstream consumers (Phase 11b, LSP/PPN Track 8, SH Track 1, PReduce). NOT pure perf gain — pays for **future expressivity** at **present perf cost**.

**C-vs-D choice is structural-vs-mechanism, not perf** — both achieve per-name wake precision; D adds structural first-class-ness on top.

#### W4 build feasibility — three options

**Option (i) — Full W4 (mutual recursion machinery) now** (~40-60 LoC)
- Pros: complete Pre-0 data
- Cons: artificial setup; doesn't reflect actual Phase 4 mechanics

**Option (ii) — W4 light (propagator wake precision) now** (~20-30 LoC) ← **My recommendation**
- Discriminating measurement: B wakes-all vs C/D wake-target-only
- Tractable scope in this session
- Captures the load-bearing data for B vs {C, D} decision

**Option (iii) — Defer to Phase 4B or 4C** (when actual Phase 4 propagators exist)
- Most realistic measurement
- Cons: variant decision happens with incomplete Pre-0 data; might require Phase 4 revisit

**Path A LOCKED — def-entry value migration split to NEW 4A.b-ii**: the design row bundled "retire add-type-only subsumed by STRUCTURAL" (= migrate cell values `(cons type value)` → `def-entry`) into 4A.b. But `def-entry-merge`'s strict set-once is a behavior CHANGE (silent double-writes → def-collision) — contradicts 4A.b's probe-diff=0 gate. Plus a ripple: loaded-module mnr cells materialize into `module-info.env-snapshot` → `.pnet`-serialized + copied to Layer-2; def-entry structs would propagate there. So: **4A.b = read-flip only, `(cons type value)` preserved, merge-replace mnr cells (exactly matches today's hash-set), probe-diff=0 clean.** **4A.b-ii (NEW) = def-entry value-shape migration** with "expect collision findings" gate (SRE Track 2I precedent) + materialization-boundary handling. 4A.a's STRUCTURAL registration sits inert-but-tracked until 4A.b-ii (honest deferral, no dual-path).

**M2 capture-gap CORRECTED (my error, caught by 4A.b audit)**: §18.16.5.M2 named only test-support(6)+batch-worker. Audit found the full `current-definition-cells-content`/`-cell-ids` parameterize set is **~8 groups across 6 files**, including **3 PRODUCTION-load-bearing sites M2 missed**: `driver.rkt:2070` (module-load), `metavar-store.rkt:1814` (with-fresh-meta-env speculation), `lsp/server.rkt:121,208` (LSP). Plus test-capability-06 + 2 benches. This is the capture-gap pattern materializing on my own M2 capture — Q-4A.5's original "~14+ sites" was right; M2's summary collapsed it. Corrected §18.16.5.M2 with a ⚠ correction block + full enumeration at §18.17.2.

**Sub-decisions**: (1) Layer-1-only flip (Layer 2 fallback stays until 4A.c); (2) add-type-only STAYS at 4A.b (retires 4A.b-ii) — simpler than thin-wrapper; (3) LSP mechanical-mirror + defer repl-session persistence to PPN Track 11.

**Methodology data points**: "Audit-First Prevents Under-Scoped Implementation" + "Capture-Gap Pattern" both materialized (the audit caught M2's under-capture before 4A.b code); "Audit-Driven Scope Expansion Wide-vs-Narrow" applied (Wide chosen). Persisted §18.17 (10 subsections) + §3 tracker rows 4A.b (Path A) + NEW 4A.b-ii.

**Drift risks D-4Ab-1..5**: incomplete site-mirroring (D-1, mitigated by §18.17.2 checklist + full-suite gate); def-entry creep into 4A.b (D-2 — Path A keeps cons); belt-and-suspenders fallback (D-3 — cells-content goes dead not dual-read); Layer-2 entanglement (D-4 — Layer-1 only); register-global-env-cells! role (D-5 — audit retirement at impl).

### 4A.b CLOSED (2026-05-28)

Read-path flip complete — per-file mnr cells are the authoritative Layer-1 read source. Full suite **8302 / 0 failures** (probe-diff=0 via suite + test-elaboration-parity).

| Commit | What |
|---|---|
| `f7f72ba8` | Commit 1: site-mirroring (~8 parameterize groups; param unread → zero behavior change) |
| `2ea570e3` | Commit 2: atomic flip — 5 readers + 3 writers → mnr; `mnr-add-or-update!` helper |

**Implementation-level findings**:
- **R1: 3 writers, not 2** — `global-env-remove!` is a 3rd writer (design row said 2). Within Wide scope.
- **R2: dispatch stays on the box + lazy-init** — writer dispatch unchanged (`current-prelude-env-prop-net-box`); only the write TARGET flips (mnr vs cells-content). `mnr-add-or-update!` lazy-inits `(or (current-file-module-network-ref) (make-module-network))` for no-wrapper contexts.
- **R3: register-global-env-cells! no-ops naturally** — once writers stop writing cells-content, its per-name loop iterates empty (dead at 4A.b, removed 4A.c). No edit needed.
- **metavar-store→namespace require**: cycle-safe (already transitive via global-env→namespace; propagator.rkt:1278 confirms propagator can't import metavar-store).

**The notable measurement event — .pnet-cold masquerading as +27% regression**: first post-flip full-suite run was 138.4s (+27% vs 108.9s at 4A.a close) — above the 1.2× regression threshold. **Investigated before committing** (didn't catalogue "suite green → done"): re-ran warm-.pnet → **115.2s (+5.8%, within variance, under the 1.2× gate)**. The +27% was one-time .pnet cache regeneration (read-flip changed elaboration → stale .pnet → rebuild), NOT a per-run regression. Lesson reinforced (testing.md "separate compile from test timing"): the first post-change run includes .pnet regen; steady-state is the warm re-run. The residual +5.8% is the expected per-lookup CHAMP-read cost (4A.0 bench predicted D reads slower than hasheq); recovery path 4B (dep-recording retirement) + §18.10.3 Candidate 2 (read-side direct-ref cache) if it matters.

**Methodology data points**:
- **"Confirm wall-time on warm-.pnet before concluding regression"** — NEW (1 data point). The first post-elaboration-change run regenerates .pnet; a +27% first-run can be ~+6% steady-state. Always re-run warm before raising a regression. Watching-list (graduate at 2-3).
- **VAG-interrogates-anomaly** — the adversarial VAG challenged the wall-time anomaly (re-ran warm) rather than cataloguing "suite green." Good instance of the challenge column doing work.

**Deferred (tracked)**: def-entry value migration → 4A.b-ii; Layer-2 (module-defs, prelude-env) + callbacks + param removal → 4A.c; LSP session-persistent mnr → PPN Track 11 (TODO comment at lsp/server.rkt:208); benches mirroring → 4A.d.

**Next**: 4A.b-ii (def-entry value migration, "expect collision findings" gate) OR 4A.c (Layer-2 + callback + param retirement + driver import-handler share-by-reference). Per §18.17.4 the natural order is 4A.b-ii then 4A.c, but 4A.c (imports migration) may be sequenced first if preferred — open at next mini-design.

### 4A.c mini-design + audit OPENED (2026-05-28, §18.18) — Path X LOCKED

User chose 4A.c next. Delegated a comprehensive code audit (agent `a39c48df0d915dc27`) per the audit-delegation pattern (architecturally-uncertain phase opening — 3rd data point, promotes the pattern). Audit surfaced **4 risks, 2 blocking** — materially reshaping 4A.c.

**The feared crux is CLEARED**: name qualification is FQN-symmetric (both the copy and the imports-cascade key on FQN; the fat transitive mnr holds exactly the FQN keys `resolve-name` produces). So share-by-reference doesn't lose name translation.

**RISK 1 (blocking) deep-dived per user questions**: `.pnet`-cached modules have `module-network = #f` (steady state = all prelude from `.pnet`). Share-by-reference would add #f → prelude invisible. Analysis: the env mnr IS serializable in principle (cell-only, no propagators, single named merge-fn) — the robust answer is **SH Track 1** (.pnet network-as-value), BUT SH Track 1 gates on PPN Track 4 (this work) → can't pivot (dependency inversion). Reconstruction shim (~15 LoC, rebuild mnr from already-serialized env-snapshot at load, no format change) is the bounded interim — a **precursor** to SH Track 1, not throwaway. Cost moves per-import-copy → once-per-module-reconstruction + O(1) imports (net win when M>1).

**Path X LOCKED (user)**: build the reconstruction shim now + do share-by-reference, even though interim. *"Share-by-reference is a strong motivator, even if temporary shim."* Rejected Path Y (scope-split, defer share-by-ref to module-loading-on-network). Q-4A.4 Option (b) timing reaffirmed.

**RISK 2 (blocking)**: `current-prelude-env` has live external consumers (`constraint-propagators:221` find-fqn-for-local-name HOT path + `cfa-analysis:112`) that iterate it as a flat FQN hash → migrate to cascade-flattened `global-env-snapshot` (perf: find-fqn needs KEYS not values → key-cascade). **RISK 3**: in-flight module's mnr needs `imports` populated at import time. **RISK 4**: defn-param-names cell is write-only → retireable unit.

**4-step partition** (audit-driven, each independently green): 4A.c-i reconstruction shim → 4A.c-ii-a global-env-snapshot cascade-flatten + RISK 2 consumer migration (safe prep) → 4A.c-ii-b share-by-reference flip + in-flight imports + lookup Layer-2 drop (probe-diff=0) → 4A.c-iii retire dead params + callbacks. Persisted §18.18 + §3 tracker rows.

**Methodology**: audit-delegation pattern 3rd data point (PROMOTE); Audit-First-Prevents-Under-Scoped materialized (audit caught 2 blocking risks + cleared the feared crux before any code); the "deletion"-framed 4A.c had hidden Cat-B architectural work (RISK 1 .pnet-mnr + RISK 2 consumer migration).

**Next**: 4A.c-i reconstruction shim (bounded, ~15 LoC, independently green).

### 4A.c-i CLOSED (2026-05-28) — `e7b11170`

`module-network-from-snapshot` (namespace.rkt) rebuilds the env mnr from a flat env-snapshot; wired at the `.pnet` load path (driver.rkt:1917, was `module-network #f`). +2 unit tests (round-trip materialize == snapshot; empty). UNUSED until 4A.c-ii-b → zero behavior change. Suite **8304 / 0 / 113.4s warm** (+4.1%, within variance; no `.pnet` regen since source unchanged).

**Count-anomaly investigation (methodology)**: first full-suite run reported 8299 (−5 vs expected 8304). Traced via timings.jsonl per-file diff to `test-properties.rkt` 13→8 — a `rackcheck` property-based test. Isolation = stable 13 (baseline); re-run full suite = 8304 (correct). Conclusion: **flaky batch-worker/rackcheck test-case reporting under parallel load**, not a regression (all-pass throughout; isolation confirms no real test lost). The "confirm before concluding" discipline (re-run, same shape as the `.pnet`-cold lesson) resolved it. **NEW watching-list candidate**: "full-suite rackcheck/property-test counts can flaky-under-report vs isolation under parallel batch load — verify count anomalies via isolation + re-run before treating as regression" (1 data point; cf. .pnet-cold-confirm-warm).

**Next**: 4A.c-ii-a (extend global-env-snapshot to flatten imports cascade + migrate RISK 2 consumers constraint-propagators:221 + cfa-analysis:112 — safe prep, pre-flip) → 4A.c-ii-b (share-by-reference flip).

## §4 The LOCKED migration recipe (T1-T4) — verbatim
Per-fixture (keeps `shared-global-env` a HASH so hash-uses survive; makes prelude an IMPORT so external-definitions-snapshot/cfa still see it):
- **T1 capture**: `(values (current-prelude-env) …)` → `(values (global-env-snapshot) …)`

- **T4 setup**: add `[current-file-module-network-ref (make-module-network)]` to the setup define-values parameterize
- **Requires**: ensure the file requires `namespace.rkt` (module-network-add-import, module-network-from-snapshot, make-module-network, current-file-module-network-ref) + `global-env.rkt` (global-env-snapshot). Most already do.

## §7 Key surprises — must NOT re-trip
1. **R-F is lazy-init, NOT parameterize** (§3/§5). Parameterize at process-file/string scopes the mnr → breaks define-then-use-after (19 failures). Confirmed by revert.
2. **The add-import correction** (§18.18.6.7 finding 2): inject must make prelude an IMPORT (`module-network-add-import (make-module-network) (module-network-from-snapshot V)`), NOT own-cells (`module-network-from-snapshot V` alone) — else external-definitions-snapshot (cfa/find-fqn, ii-a) EXCLUDES it and cfa breaks.
3. **Migration DECOUPLES from cutover** — capturing `global-env-snapshot` (a hash) works pre- and post-cutover → migrate-first stays green. Two green steps, no red commit.
4. **.pnet modules stay fat** (reconstructed via module-network-from-snapshot, empty imports) — cascade finds their defs in own cells. Accepted interim (de-fatten = SH Track 1).

### The audit (workflow `wf_75ea10c5-68a`)

7 parallel facet audits (mnr-substrate · global-env read/write · driver orchestration · cutover chokepoint · 80-fixture sweep · 3-file PILOT deep-dive · consumers/downstream/serialization) → 1 adversarial completeness critic. 8 agents, ~1.2M subagent tokens, 331 tool uses. **Verdict: NO-GO** on the §18.18.6.7 recipe + 4-action cutover as written. Every load-bearing refutation VERIFIED directly against code by the main session (R-lens) before acting.

### Root cause (verified) — one fix, three failures

`global-env-snapshot` (global-env.rkt:366) uses own-cells-only `module-network-materialize`, NOT the imports cascade. Its prelude/imports content comes entirely from the Layer-2 base, which **action 3 empties**. Contrast: `external-definitions-snapshot:404` already uses `module-network-cascade-materialize`. Post-cutover, `global-env-snapshot` collapses to thin/empty for import-only preambles. Fans out to: (1) **T1 fixture capture thins** (refutes "decoupled, stays green" — VERIFIED: all 3 PILOT preambles import-only; P2 `test-collection-fns-01.rkt:76` `(hash-count > 0)` flips RED); (2) **serialized `.pnet` mod-env thins** → transitive-dep loss, MASKED by infrastructure-stale `.pnet` invalidation (false-green); (3) **production capability inference thins** (driver.rkt:1410).

### The methodological event

The fixture-classification + PILOT-deep-dive facets returned a clean GO — they fell into **this design's signature capture-gap** (hash-shape vs hash-content; `global-env-snapshot` vs `external-definitions-snapshot`). The cross-facet **critic caught the disagreement** on the load-bearing "does T1 stay green?" question and adjudicated with ground truth (facets 2/3/6 correct). Parallel-fan-out + adversarial cross-check surfaced the gap; the main session confirmed it against code. Strongest instance yet of the audit-delegation pattern (**5th data point**).

### 4A.c-ii-b cutover — ⚠ ATTEMPTED → BLOCKED → REVERTED (§18.18.6.9)

Opened ii-b-cutover with a thorough mini-audit (grounded all 4 action sites + the R-C test-support/batch-worker reality against current code). Implemented the 4 RF-guarded actions (add-import @namespace:826, store-thin in-flight mnr @driver:2149, retire 5 copy loops, action-4 `[else #f]`); compiled clean (one paren off-by-one caught by check-parens — the original `mnr2))))` 4th paren closed the enclosing parameterize body).

**RF-4 gate caught it — process-file-first was decisive.** Acceptance file via `process-file` went **0 → 10 "Unbound variable"** (baseline confirmed 0 via git-stash). Full suite: **15 FAILURES** spanning migrated fixtures AND core module-loading (test-namespace, test-elaborator, test-prelude-system, test-foreign). The breadth → module-loading bug, not fixtures.

**ROOT CAUSE (diagnosed, verified):** action-4 (drop Layer-2) removes names the mnr cascade doesn't carry. Instrumented the lookup-miss + add-import: action-1 fires correctly, FQN lookups HIT, only **bare nullary constructors** (`zero`/`true`/`false`) miss. Decisive narrow test: restore Layer-2 fallback (keep actions 1-3) → hashable loads, `zero` 0 misses (found in L2). So `zero` resolves via Layer-2 but is **unreachable through the import cascade**. Mechanism: pre-cutover Layer-2 accumulated bare/implicit names via (a) copy-loop FLATTENING of full transitive env-snapshots into every importer's L2 (implicit access to un-imported defs) + (b) test-support's prelude-cache load with `prop-net-box=#f` → legacy global-env-add path → bare names to L2. The cascade carries only the EXPLICIT import DAG, FQN-keyed.

**Why the §18.18.6.8 reframe under-modeled it**: the 7-facet+critic audit verified EXPLICIT (FQN, import-DAG) resolution + `.pnet`/fixture fatness (RF-1), but did NOT model IMPLICIT (bare-name, transitive-flattening) resolution. RF-3's "[else #f] — cascade is sole source" is the wrong assumption: the cascade is NOT yet a complete Layer-2 replacement.

## §3 NEXT TASK — the investigation mini-audit (Option 1, USER-LOCKED)
**Pivotal question:** how do **bare nullary constructors** (`zero`, `true`, `false`) get resolved, and why does the mnr import cascade MISS them while Layer-2 had them? The answer picks the reframe.

Concrete investigation steps (do these in a fresh session):
1. **How are `data` constructors added to global-env?** When `data Nat := zero | suc Nat` elaborates, does it call `global-env-add` with BARE `zero` or FQN `prologos::data::nat::zero` (or both)? And does it register in `current-ctor-registry`? → Grep where `data`/`deftype` declarations are processed (likely `macros.rkt` preparse + `elaborator.rkt` + a data-handler; search for constructor registration + `global-env-add` calls in the data path).
2. **Does nat's mnr contain bare `zero`?** Load `prologos::data::nat`, inspect `(module-network-ref-cell-id-map mnr)` keys. BARE `zero` present → the cascade COULD reach it via the prelude import (then the fix is ensuring the import chain reaches nat / the prelude-cache puts bare names in the mnr). FQN-ONLY → bare `zero` is never in any mnr → need a different mechanism (ctor-registry resolution in the elaborator, or special-case `zero`/`true`/`false` like `suc`).
3. **Elaborator resolution path** (`elaborator.rkt:700-761`): bare name → own-FQN (703) → where-method (715) → ns-context FQN (718) → **bare `global-env-lookup-type` (729)** → multi-defn (735) → HKT (743) → **`suc` special-case (750, eta-expand)** → primitive-op (759) → **unbound (761)**. NOTE: `suc` IS special-cased at 750 but `zero`/`true`/`false` are NOT — they fall to the bare global-env lookup (729). KEY QUESTION: why is `zero` in global-env (L2) but `suc` is special-cased? Is there an asymmetry to exploit (special-case the nullary ctors too)?
4. **The two L2-population sources** (both retired/dropped by the cutover): (a) PRODUCTION — the copy loops (`driver.rkt:1857-1869` etc.) FLATTEN every loaded module's full transitive env-snapshot into the importer's `current-prelude-env`; (b) TEST — `test-support.rkt:99` loads the prelude with `current-prelude-env-prop-net-box = #f` → `global-env-add` legacy path → bare names to `current-prelude-env`. Understand whether the cascade can replicate (a)'s transitive flattening for the names that matter.

**Gate**: this is a read/diagnose mini-audit (no production edits expected) → persist findings to design §18.18.6.x → THEN a reframe mini-design → THEN re-attempt the cutover.

### 4A.c-ii-b investigation — §18.18.6.9 root-cause REFUTED; real cause = import-edge/lifecycle seam (2026-05-29, §18.18.6.10)

Ran the §3 investigation as a **dynamic audit+debug workflow** (`wf_933a019a-6e0`: 6 static facets + 3 worktree-isolated empirical probes + adversarial critic + synthesis), THEN re-verified inline on main at the correct HEAD. The workflow surfaced the structural insight + a critical defect; the inline re-verification produced the complete, correct picture.

**The big correction (verified):** §18.18.6.9's "bare nullary constructors zero/true/false" attribution is **WRONG**. zero/true/false are **parser keywords** (parser.rkt:547-549) → native AST (elaborator.rkt:856/863/864), never in any env layer; they appear in the lookup `[else]` only as benign `#f`-tolerant misses. The prior diagnosis instrumented the **wrong site** (global-env `[else]`, benign-inclusive) instead of the **fatal site** (elaborator.rkt:761 `unbound-variable-error`). The "decisive narrow test" confounded `[else]`-flow with fatal-error.

**The ACTUAL fatal set** (elaborator.rkt:761 + drop Layer-2, at HEAD 5b3f9c67): dominated by **type names** `Option`×170 / `Ordering`×12 / `List`, **library functions** `nat-eq?`×94 / `cons`/`head`/`tail`/`length`/`foldr`/`add1`/`zero?`, **trait methods** `add`/`+`. Zero `zero`/`true`/`false`. **ROOT CAUSE = the import-edge/lifecycle seam**: `process-file-inner` never binds `current-file-module-network-ref`, and `ns`/imports run in `preparse-expand-all` before any elaboration-time mnr exists → the in-flight cascade holds only the file's OWN defs; imported/prelude names live only in Layer-2.

**FIX CONFIRMED empirically**: action-1 (wire `add-import`) + bind the in-flight mnr BEFORE preparse → acceptance fatal set **10 → 0** (residual `int-hash-nat`×2, foreign/non-fatal). Validates the USER-LOCKED "complete the cutover (2 + lifecycle + 4 + foreign)" direction.

**Re-scoped classes:** A (import-edge/lifecycle — DOMINANT, fixed by action-1 + bind-before-preparse; trait-method `add` folds in once the FQN is cascade-reachable); B (foreign write-side residual — `int-hash-nat`, `foreign` defs land in L2 via preparse box=#f, not their module mnr); D (test-harness — `test-namespace` only 3 unbounds under action-4, NOT the mass failure §18.18.6.9 claimed; the prior failure likely needs action-3 copy-loop retirement; needs its own RF-4-gated verification + candidate 4). Candidates 1 & 3 (bare-vs-FQN ctor registration / ctor-registry elaborator special-case) **eliminated** — zero/true/false are not the problem.

### 4A.c-ii-b cut-prep step 1 — action-1 + lifecycle + ns-reset-imports SHIPPED (2026-05-30, §18.18.6.12, commit `6f1060b5`)

Per Stage 4 Per-Phase Protocol (mini-design ⇄ mini-audit → implement → gate). First behavior-preserving step. **Full suite 8317/0/112.1s (probe-diff=0); acceptance 0.**

**Mini-audit refinement**: action-2 (thin module mnr) DEFERRED — the full-cutover verification proved transitive reachability works with the fat 4A.c-i reconstruction mnrs; action-2 is a pure optimization, not cutover-required. So cut-prep = action-1 + lifecycle + foreign-write (not +action-2).

**3 production changes**: action-1 (process-imports-spec wire-import) + lifecycle (process-file inherit-or-create mnr bind) + ns-reset-imports (process-ns-declaration resets mnr imports, keeps own defs).

**The lifecycle journey — a 3-way tension resolved in 4 iterations** (the suite probe-diff=0 gate was decisive — caught what acceptance + targeted tests missed):
- First suite run: 7 failures. Diagnostic: my `process-file` unconditionally re-bound a fresh mnr, CLOBBERING fixtures' pre-bound snapshot mnr (run-ws). Fix: inherit-or-create → 6/7 fixed.
- 7th (test-prelude-system-02 `:no-prelude`): action-1's plain-set import-wiring ACCUMULATED across run-ns calls sharing the batch-worker's per-file mnr → a full-prelude ns leaked into a later `:no-prelude` ns. 
- Iterated the mnr lifecycle: (a) conditional-inherit → leak; (b) always-`(or)` per-call parameterize → broke shared-fixture define-then-use (parameterize reverts); (c) full ns-reset to fresh mnr → broke inline cross-string modules + define-then-use (cleared own defs); **(d) reset-imports-keep-defs ✓** — the `ns` declaration is the IMPORT-SET boundary; clear imports (the leak), keep own per-name defs (define-then-use + inline modules).
- The 3 tension cases all green: test-prelude-system-02 (import isolation), test-stdlib-03-list-05 (inline-module persistence), test-transducer-01 (define-then-use).

**Methodology data points**:
- **NEW watching-list**: "a per-file/ambient param mutated by a production code path (action-1's plain set) leaks across compilation units sharing that binding; the unit boundary (the `ns` declaration) must reset the leaking facet — and the RIGHT facet (imports, not own defs)." The mnr-lifecycle is now: per-`ns` import set + persistent own defs.
- **Full-suite probe-diff=0 is the load-bearing gate** for behavior-preserving infra steps — both bugs (clobber + leak) were invisible to acceptance + targeted tests; only the batch + `:no-prelude`/inline-module fixtures exercised them. Reinforces testing.md "full suite as regression gate."
- Diagnostic protocol ran clean across 3 fix iterations (read logs → categorize → narrow → fix); no whack-a-mole.

**Next**: cut-prep step 2 = foreign-write-(b) (handle-foreign-decl → module mnr; class-B residual int-hash-nat/rkt-*). Then ii-b-cut-flip (action-3 + action-4, RF-4-gated) → ii-b-close.

## §7 Key surprises — must NOT re-trip
1. **`zero`/`true`/`false` are parser keywords** — never env-resident; not the cutover blocker. §18.18.6.9's "bare nullary constructors" diagnosis was WRONG (it instrumented the lookup `[else]` = benign misses, not the fatal site elaborator.rkt:761).
2. **The mnr lifecycle = per-`ns` import set + persistent own defs.** ns-reset clears IMPORTS, KEEPS own defs. A full mnr reset breaks inline-modules + define-then-use; a per-call parameterize breaks shared-fixture define-then-use (reverts); conditional-inherit leaks imports across run-ns calls. reset-imports-keep-defs is the only model satisfying all 3.
3. **process-file lifecycle bind MUST be inherit-or-create**, not unconditional fresh (clobbers run-ws fixtures' snapshot mnr).
4. **Full-suite probe-diff=0 is the load-bearing gate** for behavior-preserving infra — the clobber + import-leak bugs were invisible to acceptance + targeted tests (only batch + `:no-prelude`/inline-module fixtures hit them).
5. **`.pnet` masking is real** — clean+regen `.pnet` before judging the flip (stale `.pnet` flips 0↔errors). RF-4's process-file-first + explicit `.pnet` regen is the valid gate.
6. **Workflow `isolation: 'worktree'` checked out a STALE HEAD** (`c4c753a8`, 26 commits back) during the investigation — delegated empirical work must VERIFY HEAD before trusting observations.

### 4A.c-ii-b cut-prep step 2 (foreign-write-(b)) — DESIGN via dynamic workflow experiment; GO-WITH-CHANGES; persisted §18.18.6.13; NOT implemented (2026-05-30)

**Experiment**: at user direction, ran cut-prep step 2's Stage-2→4 design methodology as a **dynamic workflow** (`wf_56ccb3a7-13d`): 4 parallel Stage-2 audit facets → Stage-3 design synthesis → 5-lens adversarial critique panel (P/R/M/S + completeness critic) → grounded-pushback verdict. 12 agents, ~1.96M subagent tokens, ~85 min wall.

**Outcome**: **Stages 2-3 (design + adversarial critique) = strong success; Stage 4 (implementation) = BLOCKED** by the workflow worktree-HEAD provisioning defect (the `isolation:'worktree'` agent was provisioned at stale base `c4c753a8` = 4A.a-1, missing 4A.b + cut-prep step 1 — **2nd occurrence** of the defect first seen at §18.18.6.10). The implementer agent diagnosed the wrong base and correctly aborted (no broken edit, no ref surgery, worktree left clean). `main` clean + untouched throughout (HEAD `e0d4bd53`).

**Verdict GO-WITH-CHANGES** — the critique materially HARDENED the gate (these are real, not cataloguing):
- **F2 (sharpest, accepted)**: the deep-proof must ISOLATE foreign-write-(b) as the SOLE mnr-population path — the original "drop just Layer-2 [else]" dry-run leaves the 5 copy loops live, so class-B→0 could be attributable to a copy loop, not the new write (recurrence of the §18.18.6.9 misdiagnosis). **This flaw was in MY OWN workflow's dry-run instruction** — the completeness critic caught it. The adversarial fan-out earning its cost.
- **S-2 (accepted)**: deep-proof must assert VALUE-EQUIVALENCE vs a pre-drop snapshot, not mere non-emptiness (shadow-promoted wrong entry would score residual=0).
- **P-1(a)/R-8 (accepted)**: gate the write on an ALREADY-BOUND mnr `(when (current-file-module-network-ref) …)` — prevents the orphan-mnr + dep-edge provenance flip in bare process-string; strictly more Correct-by-Construction; deletes the "one nuance" the draft excused.
- **F1/S-3 (accepted)**: collision audit for `+`/`add1`/`sub1`/`zero?` (single-writer-per-name is a load-bearing non-monotone assumption).
- **P-2/F4 (accepted)**: build the class-B set MECHANICALLY (instrument handle-foreign-decl), not from the stale 10-name list.
- **box-independence**: must call `mnr-add-or-update!` (box-free, :256), never box-gated `global-env-add` (:275; box=#f at preparse → silent no-op).
- Re-verified all load-bearing code claims against HEAD `e0d4bd53` myself (the implementer verified against the wrong base). 4-edit spec grounded.

**Persisted §18.18.6.13** (critique-hardened mini-design + hardened gate + 16 finding dispositions + 9 drift risks + status). **Design CLOSED; NOT implemented** — user chose persist-design-now, implement-later (main session, sidestepping the worktree defect).

**Worktree cleanup**: removed the experiment's `wf_56ccb3a7-13d-12` worktree + branch. 5 pre-existing worktrees remain (`agent-aadecfd2`, `crazy-merkle-cbee28`, `nifty-elbakyan-bdc91b`, `objective-black-8a24b4`, `objective-keller-9407ad`) — each clean but 3 commits ahead of main + not created this session; surfaced to user (not removed — possible concurrent sessions).

**Methodology data points (watching-list)**:
- **Workflow worktree-HEAD provisioning defect — 2nd data point** (§18.18.6.10 was the 1st). `isolation:'worktree'` does NOT provision at main's HEAD; delegated empirical work must verify HEAD. Graduation-worthy (the defect blocks the Stage-4 half of any design+implement workflow).
- **Adversarial fan-out catches the author's own design flaws** (the F2 isolation gap was in the workflow's own dry-run instruction) — strongest single argument FOR the workflow-as-critique-engine pattern. 1 data point.
- **Disproportionality**: ~1.96M tokens for a ~4-line change. Rigor was the point (user opted in), but a lighter panel suffices for routine work; the heavy panel earns its cost on adversarial-critique-heavy or architecturally-uncertain steps.
- **Methodology tension realized**: the mini-design that's normally co-designed with the user ran autonomously; the user reviewed at the END (verdict had zero manufactured open-questions — good — but co-design input was front-run).

**Reflection on the workflow-as-methodology-engine experiment pending** (user request) → joint decision on principles/methodology/rules updates afterward. NOT updated yet.

Next: reflect on the workflow experiment → decide principle/methodology/rules updates → then main-session implementation of foreign-write-(b) + hardened gate.**

### Dynamic-workflow process codifications LANDED (2026-05-30)

After joint reflection + discussion, codified the three dynamic-workflow disciplines (user agreed all points):
- **`.claude/rules/workflow.md`** — "Dynamic-workflow code-state discipline" (pin + verify HEAD SHA; the silent-divergence hazard: worktree agents on a stale base while absolute-path readers resolve to main) + "Diff-back, don't land-in-workflow" (main session is the single source of code-state truth; novel-design implementation is categorically main-session; only verified-base mechanical migration may implement in-workflow).
- **`DESIGN_METHODOLOGY.org` § "Delegation vs Co-Design (Dynamic Workflows)"** — the organizing principle (multiple-independent-minds → workflow; one-shared-evolving-understanding → conversational); the good-fit list (research sweeps / mini-audits / adversarial critique rounds / mechanical migration / completeness sweeps / parity verification); the decision-boundary guardrail (workflow produces material, human+main-session decides + lands); the scale tiers (0/1/2, start-lean-escalate); high-stakes mini-design openings = fan out audit + pre-critique, keep Q-resolution in dialogue.
- **`DEVELOPMENT_LESSONS.org` § "Dynamic Workflow Is the Generalization of Audit-Delegation — Amplifier, Not Replacement"** — the core positive (adversarial agent caught the workflow author's own design flaw) + the 3 cautions (worktree-HEAD defect 2nd data point; disproportionality; co-design tension).

Meta: this discussion was itself the co-design we're protecting — deliberately NOT run as a workflow despite the keyword trigger (running a workflow to decide how to use workflows would refute the codification). foreign-write-(b) implementation still ⬜ (main-session, when we return to it).

🔖 **Process codifications landed (workflow.md + DESIGN_METHODOLOGY + DEVELOPMENT_LESSONS). foreign-write-(b) still designed-not-implemented. Suite unchanged (docs only).**

Implemented §18.18.6.13 in the main session per Stage 4 Per-Phase Protocol (mini-design ⇄ mini-audit → implement → hardened gate). 3 edits, +38 LoC, all-additive.

- **Mini-audit** (vs HEAD `e0d4bd53`): all 4 edit sites + R-10 confirmed (mnr-add-or-update! box-free @256; global-env-add box-gated @275; driver foreign sites @2538/2543; driver requires global-env.rkt plainly). Zero reframe.
- **Edits**: `global-env-add-to-mnr!` (box-INDEPENDENT, P-1(a) already-bound gate) + provide; bare + FQN additive calls in handle-foreign-decl. Box-independence is load-bearing (reuse of box-gated global-env-add would no-op at preparse).
- **Gate ALL PASS**: full suite **8318/0/114.9s** (= 8317 + 1 new test; probe-diff=0; warm ≈ baseline, no perf regression); collision audit PASS (all foreign decls `:as`-aliased; 3 module-scoped name-collisions value-appropriate); **deep-proof A/B DECISIVE** (cascade query = post-cut-flip path; ON → foreign in Layer-1 cascade bare+FQN, value-equivalent to Layer-2; OFF → cascade empty → load-bearing + F2-isolated + not belt-and-suspenders).

**The one surprise (the gate earned its keep)**: test-foreign failed 2 alias "original-hidden" tests. Diagnosed (probe, instrument-and-revert) as **cross-test mnr leakage** — batch-worker binds one mnr per file; test-foreign's bespoke run-ns reset Layer-2 per call but not the mnr, so foreign-write-(b) leaked an earlier bare-`add1` into a later alias test. **NOT a production bug** (one process-file = one unit; alias-hiding holds — we write the alias, not the original). Fix: run-ns resets the mnr per call (extracted `call-in-ns-env`). **Confirms critique F3/R-6** (batch-worker mnr-bind extent) — same shape as §18.18.6.12's ns-reset insight, at the test-harness layer.

**Made the deep-proof permanent**: new regression test `foreign/write-b-mnr-cascade-reachable` (+1) — foreign def reachable via mnr cascade (bare+FQN) value-equivalently to Layer-2; guards against silent foreign-write-(b) regression before the cut-flip relies on it.

**Methodology data points**:
- **Behavior-preserving full-suite gate caught a real test-isolation gap** invisible to targeted tests + acceptance (only the batch + the bespoke-run-ns file exercised it). Reinforces testing.md "full suite as regression gate."
- **Diagnostic protocol clean** (read log → reproduce in isolation via probe → narrow to the shared-mnr leak → fix → re-verify); no whack-a-mole. The instrument-and-revert deep-proof + the isolation probe were the decisive empirical tools (Empirical Falsification as Audit Complement).
- **The workflow's GO-WITH-CHANGES design held up under implementation**: every accepted critique change (P-1(a) gate, box-independence, bare+FQN, value-equivalence, F2-isolation, collision audit) was load-bearing; F3/R-6 (disposed as "Stage-4 verify") materialized exactly as flagged. The design+critique workflow → main-session implementation handoff worked as the codification intends.

Per Stage 4. The **behavior-changing** sub-phase (vs -a's dead-code). Mini-design + mini-audit were the load-bearing part: the crux (learned from the §18.18.6.9 cutover blocker) is "does the cascade supply everything the Layer-2 base did?" — I grounded it in code, did NOT assume.

**Decisive mini-audit finding:** enumerated WRITERS of `current-prelude-env` / `current-module-definitions-content` — **NONE in production** (only a comment + a bench doc-comment matched). Post-cut-flip + a1, both are bound `(hasheq)` and never written → **EMPTY in production**. So dropping the Layer-2 base from the 4 readers is **behavior-PRESERVING in production**; the behavior change is confined to test-module-network-01's artificially-seeded fixtures. This collapsed the §18.18.6.9 "is-the-cascade-complete?" risk from "scary unknown" to "verify the empty-base claim empirically" (which the RF-4 gate did).

**Changed (global-env.rkt):** `global-env-snapshot` → `module-network-cascade-materialize` only; `global-env-names` → `module-network-cascade-names` only; `external-definitions-snapshot`/`-names` → imports-cascade only (Path-Y exclude-local preserved); `global-env-remove!` → mnr-only. Refreshed the stale "retires at 4A.c-ii-b" comments. **After -b, Layer-2 has ZERO production readers** → -c retires the params cleanly.

**Test (test-module-network-01.rkt:280-346):** rewrote the 5 reader cases to cascade-only + ADDED an RF-5 **regression guard** — a seeded `current-prelude-env 'p1` asserted ABSENT from `global-env-snapshot` (guards against re-introducing a Layer-2 base read; the behavior change captured as a test).

**RF-4 gate (behavior-changing → not suite-green-alone):** clean `.pnet` regen + process-file acceptance = **0 errors** (61 forms; production cascade-only path verified — capability-inference driver:1410 + module-mnr build driver:2128 both consume the cascade-only snapshot) + full suite **8318/0/102.0s** incl. the 8-file capability cohort. The cascade-completeness assumption VERIFIED, not assumed.

**Methodology data points:**
- **Audit "enumerate the writers, not just the readers" de-risked a behavior change** — the scary "is the cascade complete?" question (the §18.18.6.9 trap shape) dissolved once the audit showed the Layer-2 base has zero production writers (→ empty → dropping it is a no-op in production). Cheaper + more decisive than tracing every consumer. Watching-list candidate: "to de-risk dropping a fallback layer, enumerate its WRITERS — an unwritten layer is empty, so dropping it is behavior-preserving regardless of readers."
- **RF-4 process-file-first gate (clean .pnet) earned its keep again** as the behavior-change verifier (acceptance 0 on the production cascade-only path before trusting the suite).

The cleanly-dead set is the OTHER three: `current-module-definitions-content` + `current-definition-cell-ids` + `current-defn-param-names-cell-id` (zero readers anywhere incl. LSP, verified). Sweep is 263 files binding `[current-prelude-env ...]` but only 3 non-`(hasheq)` seeds (batch-worker `ready-global-env`, the -b regression guard, the LSP :208).

**Empirical confirmation (the reframe driver):** a `module->namespace` probe (no production edit) drove `eval-in-session-raw!` across commands → `cmd1 "def x := 42"` ok; **`cmd2 "x"` → Unbound variable x**; `cmd4 "def y := 7\ny"` (one command) ok. **Interactive editing (cross-command user defs) has been BROKEN since 4A.b** — the mnr is fresh-per-command + discarded (no `repl-session` mnr field), and the param-mirror the session persists went vestigial when 4A.b flipped `global-env-add` to always-mnr. It worked pre-4A.b → it is a regression 4A.b introduced. (Probe nuance: `cmd3 "[int+ 1 2]"` survives because `int+` is an elaborator primitive-op, not mnr/global-env resolution — not a cross-command-cascade test.)

**Reframe (user-approved):** split -c → **-c** (narrow: the 3 cleanly-dead params + harness sweep + `lint --strict`) + **NEW 4A.c-iii-d** (restore interactive editing — add a `repl-session` mnr field, `eval-in-session-raw!` inherit-or-create + writeback the mnr, rework hover/completion to resolve via the session mnr, retire the last 2 Layer-2 params, + ADD the cross-command regression test the probe seeds). Framed as a **regression-fix** (4A.c owns the breakage it introduced; a bounded slice of Track 11 pulled forward because we caused it + hold the context) — NOT scope-creep. Persisted §18.18.7.c-reframe + Q1 partition (3→4) + §3 tracker.

**Principle posture:** Correct-by-Construction (persist the live store, not vestigial param-snapshots) + Completeness (don't ship the regression behind a defer). Q4's "LSP/REPL → Track 11" rested on the false premise that the LSP worked via the params; the probe refutes it → the defer is reframed with the refutation NAMED, not silently overridden.

**Methodology data points:**
- **Conversational co-design beats the options menu** (user-directed) — the menu flattened the (A)/(B) tradeoff and hid the decisive fact (the regression). Exploring conversationally + adversarially surfaced it. Reverting to how we've always worked.
- **Audit-Precedes-Implementation REFRAMING + Empirical-Falsification-as-Audit-Complement** — the mini-audit (current-prelude-env LSP-bound) + the probe (cross-command broken) reshaped the partition BEFORE any -c code. The §18.18.6.9 "verify, don't assume" lesson applied: the probe turned "I think interactive editing is broken" into proof.
- **"Enumerate the writers" + "drive the actual entry point"** were the two decisive empirical moves (writer-census → base empty at -b; `eval-in-session-raw!` probe → cross-command broken at -c). Tracing alone would have left both as assumptions.

### lint-parameters baseline drift — RESOLVED (2026-06-01, orthogonal to 4A.c)

The -c gate surfaced that `lint-parameters --strict` had been silently failing on 16 pre-existing unbaselined params. Worked it through per the principle-challenged plan (B + triage + capture), kept SEPARATE from 4A.c.

**What it is**: the lint is a tactical static guard against two-context-boundary parameter leakage (longitudinal pattern 7). It classifies each `make-parameter` as private / test-registered / unclassified, and `--strict` flags unclassified params not in `parameter-lint-baseline.txt`. 16 had accumulated NEW (unbaselined) across recent tracks — the guard rusted (it isn't run in pre-commit/CI).

**Triage (the judgment step)**: checked cross-module usage of all 16 → every one has 3–68 cross-module uses → none cleanly private-izable; and they're per-command-reset (`reset-meta-store!`) / per-elaboration (`current-source-loc`) state, not per-test-isolation params → test-registering would mis-classify. So **all 16 are irreducible exported state → genuine PM Track 12 (params→cells) agenda; no avoidable debt**. The triage found nothing to classify away — itself the finding.

**Resolution**: `--save-baseline` (own commit, separate from -c) accepted the 16 as known-debt AND cleaned **18 stale entries** for params retired by earlier tracks (`current-speculation-stack`, the S2.e-iv-c meta-store/champ-box cluster, `current-retracted-assumptions`, etc.) → baseline now accurate (186 = current unclassified); `--strict` GREEN. Captured the 16 as PM Track 12 cell-migration agenda in DEFERRED.md; reframed the gate honestly ("no NEW unclassified" not "clean"). NOT laundered into -c (separate honest commit + capture, per the "don't launder pre-existing debt" principle).

**Principle posture**: the deep fix (cells, Correct-by-Construction) is PM Track 12 — named + tracked, not pretended-fixed. The baseline is used as its documented purpose (accepted-known-debt register), not as a "fix." Restored the NEW-detection signal (a permanently-red gate would mask future real drift — the lint's whole purpose). Rejected: leave-red (defeats the guard), classify-now (scope-creep into PM Track 12), retire-the-lint (premature, before the structural fix lands).

**Meta data point**: a static guard that isn't operationalized (run automatically) rusts silently — 16 params slipped past `--strict` across ~5 tracks because nothing ran it. Wiring `--strict` into pre-commit/CI is a small follow-up (flagged in DEFERRED); else it re-rusts. The "Validated ≠ Deployed" shape applied to a GUARD: a guard not run is a guard not deployed.

Justification consistency: -d exists because "4A.c owns the breakage 4A.b introduced" — 4A.b broke file-save hover/completion identically, so the SAME justification covers :546; excluding it is arbitrary. Correct-by-Construction + Completeness + gate-purity.

## 4A.b-ii SHIPPED (`39f7f48e`, 2026-06-01) — def-entry STRUCTURAL value shape deployed (LWW merge)

Opened the next natural sub-phase (4A.b-ii — the deferred Path-A def-entry value migration) per Stage 4. A re-grounding audit (3 read-only HEAD-pinned Agent facets + main-session R-lens at HEAD `d8485097`) + a user policy clarification RESHAPED it before any code.

### The reframe (the methodology event — deepest of the arc)
4A.b-ii was conceived (§18.15.5/§18.17.4) around def-entry's set-once `:value` merge — the "expect collision findings" gate (catch double-write-with-inconsistency). The user clarified **re-definitions are LEGAL last-write-wins** by current language-design intent (whether that's right is a separate, OPEN discussion). The merge sees only old+new values — it can't distinguish a legal redefinition from an internal double-write → **set-once is wrong under the policy** (would flag `def x:=1; def x:=2` as collision). So: def-entry-merge → LWW; **gate → probe-diff=0** (the "expect collision findings" framing is moot — the GATE ITSELF FLIPPED); def-collision kept as the UNREACHABLE forward-compat ⊤ (re-activates if the language-design discussion later makes redefinition an error). Surfaced + co-designed BEFORE code (Audit-Precedes-Implementation REFRAMING applied to a DESIGN/POLICY assumption, not a code fact). User confirmed the lean to proceed (deploy the STRUCTURAL substrate; "in line with prior design decisions").

### Audit findings (verified)
- **Adapter, not through (0 production ripple)**: encapsulate def-entry in the cell; convert at the mnr read boundary. Option B (def-entry-through) = 13 deconstruct sites with SILENT `(pair?)`-guard failures (a def-entry fails pair? → bot branch → capabilities seeded as bot, foreign marshallers un-relinked) + the .pnet def-entry→raw-vector corruption (def-entry not registered in pnet-serialize).
- **Refinement (cleaner than the design's literal "writers write def-entry")**: make the mnr WRITE API symmetrically wrap cons→def-entry too → ALL callers UNCHANGED. Final diff: **3 files; global-env.rkt UNCHANGED**. "The adapter principle applied symmetrically."
- **Pure-leaf bonus**: dropping type-unify (LWW `:type` new-wins) makes definition-entry.rkt a PURE LEAF (no type-lattice require) → namespace.rkt can require it directly, INCIDENTALLY resolving the 4A.a M1 (γ) namespace→type-lattice cycle that forced the leaf-module split.
- **Materialization/.pnet boundary (re-grounded post-4A.c)**: §18.17.4's Layer-2 leg is GONE (cut-flip); the adapter at materialize/cascade-materialize keeps the .pnet/module-info.env-snapshot boundary unchanged (def-entry never reaches .pnet).

### 4A.d SHIPPED (`d15ba853`, 2026-06-01) — A-prod reworked; Q2(iv) sweep VINDICATED the measure-don't-assume call

Implemented the Variant-A rework per §18.19. Wired in the 4 dangling global-env imports + added the namespace mnr-API `only-in`.

**The Q2(iv) sensitivity sweep VINDICATED the (iv) decision (the headline event):**

| N=50, HIT path | with-elab | no-elab |
|---|---|---|
| local (0 imports) | 352 ns | 114 ns |
| import-1 | 361 ns | 127 ns |
| import-39-early | 367 ns | 146 ns |
| **import-39-late (38 misses)** | **1276 ns** | **1082 ns** |

**3.6× spread (with-elab) / 9.5× (no-elab).** A single-fat-import bench (my originally-leaned (ii)) would have reported ~360 ns and silently UNDER-REPORTED late-import hits by 3.6× — the exact bench-strawman the 4A.0 arc burned once. Measuring the sensitivity (Empirical-Falsification-as-Audit-Complement) rather than assuming "import-count doesn't matter for hits" caught a real, large effect. `local`/`import-1`/`import-39-early` cluster (~352-367 ns); the for/or walk cost only bites when a name resolves LATE in the import list. **This is the strongest validation yet of the user's "a one-line lean isn't enough" pushback** — the full alternatives + adversarial principles challenge (bench-strawman risk) drove (iv), and the measurement proved (ii) would have been a strawman.

**Headline (local-def hit, mnr cascade), regenerated:** no-elab 89/113/125 ns (N=10/50/200); with-elab 323/343/355 ns. **Honest framing (Finding 4 confirmed):** the regenerated with-elab (~343-355 ns) is HIGHER than the stale-on-disk 267-281 ns — different machinery (mnr cascade + def-entry unwrap vs the retired 3-layer param walk), both "~status-quo per-lookup". NOT chasing 280 ns; NOT showing the 2.5× (the ~230 ns with/no-elab delta IS the dep-recording cost 4B retires).

### Branch-drift caught + clean FF recovery (2026-06-01)

**Finding**: the entire 4A.d + 4A-VAG arc was committed onto **`fix/bench-executors-mult-meta-store`** — the branch the spawned bench-executors chip created — NOT onto `main`. The chip session (started mid-4A.d) branched `fix/...` from `077e9db7` (my 4A.d mini-design commit), committed the bench-executors fix `6a8fb398`, and the MAIN worktree ended up on that branch; my subsequent 4 commits (d15ba853 / 96ded87f / 27ad9708 / 77f35d4c) stacked on it. `main` sat stuck at `077e9db7`. I never re-checked `git branch` after the chip ran (the handoff said "branch main" and I trusted it across the concurrent-session event).

**Recovery (clean)**: `main` was a strict ANCESTOR of the fix-branch tip (zero divergence, `HEAD..main` empty) → a clean fast-forward. Verified zero overlap between the 5-commit fileset and the 14 user-managed uncommitted M files (so `checkout main` was safe), then `git checkout main` + `git merge --ff-only` → main = `77f35d4c`. No merge commit, no conflicts, user-managed changes preserved. Confirmed content (global-env-import-module retired = comment not define; bench-executors chip fix present, both compile) + deleted the now-merged fix branch (`-d`). main is 8 ahead of origin (don't push).

**Methodology data point (watching-list)**: **re-verify `git branch --show-current` after any spawned-task chip / concurrent session touches the repo** — a chip can leave the main worktree on its feature branch, and subsequent commits land there silently. A whole phase-arc drifted off `main` unnoticed. Cheap guard: check the branch at each sub-phase open (or before the first commit of a session). The clean-FF recovery worked here only because there was zero divergence; had `main` advanced independently, it'd have been a real merge. (NB: the 5 pre-existing `.claude/worktrees/` worktrees + OCapN/feature branches are unrelated concurrent work, already surfaced earlier — left untouched; out of scope for this merge.)

## Probe 1 DONE — residuation-fire trace; Q-4B.1 largely answered (2026-06-02, §18.21.8)

Ran Probe 1 main-session (static trace + 6 `process-file` fixtures @ `e5ede4b2`). **The key measurement = `meta_created` per fixture:**

| Fixture | Result | meta_created |
|---|---|---|
| A forward `def b := a` no-spec | ERROR (a unbound) | 0 |
| B mutual WITH specs | ERROR (b unbound in defn a) | 0 |
| C mutual NO specs | ERROR ×3 | 4 |
| D self-ref `defn loop [x] [loop x]` | WORKS | 2 |
| E backward all-spec'd ordered | WORKS | **0** |
| F backward no-spec ordered | WORKS | 4 |

**Findings:** (1) a name reference is **meta-free** — it resolves (`infer` returns the env type, `typing-core.rkt:427`) or **errors** (`expr-error`); it never residuates-with-a-meta. (2) Metas come from **un-spec'd signature INFERENCE** (E spec'd=0 vs F inferred=4, identical structure), per-defn within a command — NOT from references. (3) **`spec` does NOT populate the env type** a reference reads (B failed despite specs); the env binding is created only by the defn — a current GAP.

**Seam verdict (Q-4B.1):** the **spec→defn gate is value-level + seam-free** — provided 4B adds an **up-front type-registration ordering pass** (generalize the self-ref `global-env-add-type-only` that makes D work) so bodies type in one pass (refs resolve meta-free), and reduction un-sticks on pending values (`whnf` stuck-on-`#f`, `reduction.rkt:3018`) on the **persistent mnr**. No typing re-entry, no metas-from-residuation, no seam. **The seam bites only inference-only mutual recursion** (un-spec'd HM let-rec cycle).

**Design reframe (Probe-1-supported):** residuation = **two mechanisms** — (1) type-registration ORDERING pass (topology; Option-3 fit; surfaced sub-task: wire `spec`→env-type-cell), (2) value-level residuation propagator on the persistent mnr. Inference-cycles = the named/deferred seam.

**Persisted** §18.21.8 (results + reframe) + Q-4B.1 (→ largely answered) + §18.21.6 (Probe 1 ✅). **Remaining measurement work captured:** Probe 1b (dependent-type-on-pending-value, UNPROBED — the one case value-pending could block typing); Probe 2 (accumulation, NEXT); future validate-the-ordering-pass-when-built.

**Methodology:** empirical falsification refined the hypothesis decisively (Fixture B's with-spec FAILURE + the E-vs-F meta contrast were the falsifiers — measurement over assumption). No `.rkt` touched (fixtures in `/tmp/probe1/`); suite untouched.

**NEXT: Probe 2** — accumulation on the persistent mnr (the Track 4A "network soup" measurable; do P1/P2/P3 + inertness keep growth sub-linear).

## Probe 2 DONE — accumulation on the persistent mnr; Q-4B.2 answered (2026-06-03, §18.21.9)

Synthetic persistent prop-network accumulating N fire-once residuation propagators across N "commands" (+ isolation 2b). **Measurements (N=1000):**

| Variant | per-cmd quiescence first50→last50 | growth | mem/cmd |
|---|---|---|---|
| no self-clean | 0.017→0.383 ms | **22×** | 717 B |
| self-clean (P2) | 0.016→0.365 ms | 23× | **159 B** |
| **write-only (2b)** | 0.0026→0.001 ms | **0.4× FLAT** | — |

**Findings:** (1) network-soup cost is REAL — per-command quiescence grows ~22×/1000 cmds (≈O(N²)/file); confirms §3.3. (2) The cost is in **`run-to-quiescence`, NOT the cells** — write-only (no quiescence) is FLAT (CHAMP write is O(1)-ish). (3) NOT propagator-count-bound — P2 self-clean cut MEMORY 4.5× but not TIME (time scales with persistent network SIZE).

**Key design connection:** **4C's all-at-once install IS the network-soup mitigation** (one file-level quiescence O(N) vs per-command O(N²)) — so 4B↔4C couple more tightly than "4B then 4C" implied; the residuation props on the persistent mnr want one-quiescence-per-file. + P2 for memory. File-bounded (mnr resets per file → typical files negligible; large libs matter).

**Persisted** §18.21.9 + Q-4B.2 (answered) + §18.21.6 (Probe 2 ✅). **NEW user-raised scope: Q-4B.9** — can the scheduler be O(network-diff) instead of O(network-size)? → investigating the actual O(N) op in run-to-quiescence (§18.21.10). No `.rkt` touched (bench in `/tmp/probe2*.rkt`); suite untouched.

**Methodology:** isolation (2b write-only FLAT) pinned the cost to the scheduler — measurement over assumption, again. The two probes together: Probe 1 → spec→defn is value-level/seam-free (type-ordering + value-residuation); Probe 2 → viable on the persistent mnr but wants 4C all-at-once + P2.

**NEXT (user-directed): investigate Q-4B.9** — the O(network-size) scheduler cost + whether O(network-diff) (incremental/semi-naive) is achievable + semantics-preserving (CALM); consider bringing into scope.

### Q-4B.9 DIAGNOSED — scheduler O(network-size) → O(network-diff) (2026-06-03, §18.21.10)

Read the BSP hot path + ran diagnostics (Probe 2c GC%, 2d worklist/per-op split). **ROOT CAUSE FOUND:** `fire-and-collect-writes` (`propagator.rkt:2858-2909`) — when a fire changes the cells CHAMP, the `undeclared-writes` (:2869-2890) + `new-cells` (:2894-2909) computations each **`champ-fold` over ALL N cells** → O(N log N)/fire. The declared `value-writes` are already O(diff); the two catch-all scans are the O(N). Confirmed by elimination (worklist=2/O(1), add-prop flat, bulk-merge O(writes), GC 1%).

**O(diff) IS achievable:** the result shares structure with the snapshot → an **eq?-pruned CHAMP structural diff** (recurse only where sub-trie refs differ by eq?) is O(changed). Semantics-preserving (CALM), scheduler-layer (orthogonal to 4B). Feasibility checked: CHAMP exposes node structure (`champ-node` champ.rkt:54) but has NO diff primitive → the clean fix = a NEW ~40-80 LoC primitive + tests. A few-line PARTIAL (guard `new-cells` on `(= snapshot-next-id result-next-id)`) gives ~2× but stays O(N) (undeclared-writes remains). Core scheduler → full-suite + Probe-2 gate.

**Persisted** §18.21.10 (diagnosis + 3 fix options + gate) + Q-4B.9 (diagnosed) + the durable bench `benchmarks/micro/bench-scheduler-accumulation.rkt`.

## Scheduler O(diff) — mini-design CLOSED (design LOCKED, §7) + D-S.3 investigated (2026-06-03)

Co-designed the `champ-diff` primitive with the user (adversarial-per-choice). All decisions LOCKED → doc **§7**:
- **S.1 (b) general** `champ-diff` (handles collision nodes) — on **Correct-by-Construction**, not reusability. Adversarial finding: the audit's "collisions unreachable for cell-id CHAMPs" is **unverified** — the trie uses only the low 35 hash bits, so namespaced cell-ids (`(ns<<32)|local`, ns≥8 → bit ≥35) *can* collide. Don't bake in the unverified assumption.
- **S.2 retire the production folds** (one path) — user's CbC point. The parity oracle is a **test-local naive model** (model-based testing), NOT a production fork. Chose **(i) permanent** standing differential guard.
- **S.3 ASSERT (debug-mode)** + regression test — see below. (I'd proposed "perpetuate + defer"; user rightly rejected deferring a suspected bug unexamined. Investigating refuted my framing.)
- **S.4 straight to Option 2** (Completeness).
- cleanup: fix the stale comment propagator.rkt:2170-2171.

**D-S.3 investigation** (`wf_28e9d770-ad6`, 4 facets + critic; load-bearing facts main-session R-lens-verified — incl. catching a FACET that was factually wrong about `sre-maybe-decompose` being dead). **The drop splits by CHAMP:**
- `cell-decomps`+`pair-decomps`: **structurally unreachable in-fire** — written only by decomposition, which is dispatcher-gated (in-fire → emit topology request; actual write in a `#:tier 'topology` handler between rounds on the canonical net). **Linchpin (verified): `current-bsp-fire-round? #t` is set at EXACTLY one site, propagator.rkt:2844.**
- `cell-domains`: **reachable-by-primitive, unreachable-by-current-callers** — written by `net-new-cell` itself, which IS allowed+captured in-fire; no current production fire-fn allocates a domain-carrying cell mid-fire. A caller-invariant, not structural impossibility. **My "structurally impossible" intuition rested on a STALE comment** (propagator.rkt:2170-2171, contradicted by the CALM contract :17-22).
- **Impact if ever triggered: harmless** (wasteful-but-correct; cell-domains self-heals — re-derivable from the captured merge-fn; sole consumer is a debug-lint). No corruption path.
- → **Not a live bug, not structurally impossible — a latent gap guarded by convention.** ASSERT (debug-mode, at the new-cells capture point) makes the invariant structural + loud, zero production cost, behavior-preserving — addresses it *now* without speculative capture machinery.

**Methodology data point**: "perpetuate + defer" was the *Hot-Load-is-protocol / "we'll come back to it"* anti-pattern; the user's push to investigate surfaced (a) it isn't broken now, (b) my reason for thinking it couldn't break was a stale comment, (c) the principled move is to make the invariant structural, not defer. Investigate-before-deferring.

**Sub-phase partition** (§7.5): **S-a** (champ-diff + test-champ-diff.rkt green in isolation — NEXT) → **S-b** (rewire fire-and-collect-writes; delete 2 folds; debug ASSERT; stale-comment fix) → **S-c** (gate). State: design-lock commit (doc + dailies); production `.rkt` still `27ad9708`; suite 8327/0; branch `main`; don't push.

🔖 **Resume: S-a — write `champ-diff` in champ.rkt (+ provides) + `tests/test-champ-diff.rkt` (permanent differential oracle + forced-collision + namespaced-id + dependents-only + no-change eq? + deep tries); green in isolation. Then S-b rewire, S-c gate. Design LOCKED §7. Production `27ad9708`; branch `main`; suite 8327/0; don't push.**

## Scheduler O(diff) S-a SHIPPED — champ-diff primitive + test green in isolation (2026-06-03)

S-a per §7.5. `champ-diff` (eq?-pruned parallel CHAMP walk; `(champ-diff snap result same?) → (values changed new)`) added to champ.rkt + exported; `tests/test-champ-diff.rkt` is the **permanent differential oracle** (S.2 (i)) + edge cases. **12 tests green** in isolation:
- 450 randomized oracle trials (ordinary trie / heavy-collision hashmod-4 / mixed) — `champ-diff` ≡ naive full-scan model (the shape the production folds use);
- forced collision nodes (new + changed entries);
- the **D-S.1 adversarial case**: namespaced high-bit keys agreeing on the low-35-bits (hash 0 vs 2^35) → collision node, diffed correctly — empirically confirms collisions ARE reachable, vindicating the general-not-assert choice;
- dependents-only change (caller `same?` compares value only → not reported);
- no-change eq? fast path (diff against self / eq?-value re-insert → empty);
- deep/wide O(changed) (500-entry map, returns exactly the 5 deltas, nothing else).

champ.rkt compiles clean. Design: value-agnostic via caller-supplied `same?` (champ.rkt stays prop-cell-unaware — layering); reports result-side adds/changes only (precondition result⊇snap, documented); persistent-vs-persistent precondition documented (assert at the S-b call site, not inside the O(N)-defeating primitive). **No production caller yet → suite untouched (8327/0).**

## PPN 4C Addendum Phase 4B — decision-gate 4B.0 CLOSED: D1-D6 + Q-4B.8 partition LOCKED (2026-06-04, §18.21.16/.17)

Resumed 4B phase-design. Grounded the open questions via TWO read-only workflows, then co-designed the decisions main-session.

### The two workflows (read-only; main-session R-lens-verified)
- **grounding-audit** (`wf_2cf3f25f-dad`, 6 HEAD-pinned facets + completeness critic @ `78ac233b`, ~898K subagent tokens). Covered all open Q's (factory surface, residuation/general-body, dep-recording retirement, install-mechanism/cell-dependents, 4B↔4C↔4D coupling, Probe-1b/§6). The completeness critic caught the recurring "design enumeration under-counts" failure mode. Main-session surgically re-confirmed the 5 load-bearing reframes against code.
- **design-options-panel** (`wf_1c96bbf0-f3d`, NEW reusable `.claude/workflows/design-options-panel.js`, 6 clusters × propose→adversarial-critique + cross-cluster synthesis, ~1.68M subagent tokens). Propose agents grounded in DESIGN_PRINCIPLES/on-network/structural-thinking; critique agents in CRITIQUE_METHODOLOGY/DESIGN_METHODOLOGY Stage 3. Produced options + tradeoffs + adversarial verdicts as co-design MATERIAL — the synthesis composed the surviving leans into ONE coherent architecture + a natural partition.

### The 5 reframes (grounding's payoff — main-session-confirmed against code)
1. **Factory scope narrower than framed** — structural forms (data/trait/impl/spec) are L0 preparse-CONSUMED (macros.rkt:2482-2716), NOT process-command arms; 8 forms dual-dispatched. Factory scope = L1+L2 def/defn mnr-writers.
2. **A3-narrow's OMITTED deliverable: "NET-1 must become DRIVEN"** — the mnr installs ZERO propagators today (grep-confirmed); δ residuation can't fire without a run-to-quiescence loop on it.
3. **Dep-recording is retire-outright** — zero production consumers; dep-edges write-only (4 test readers, confirmed); the "registry IS the dep graph" granularity gap (no name in propagator/dependent structs) but nothing consumes name-granular edges.
4. **Tier lean REFUTED** — fork-on-union (live precedent) is `#:tier 'value`, not topology.
5. **Probe-1b retirable** — dependent-type-on-pending-value is STUCK-NOT-BLOCK; the real risk is intra-fixpoint ORDERING (4C-era).

### Decisions LOCKED (co-designed, grounded)
- **D1 — §6 NOT a 4B blocker** (accept-with-tripwire + structural guard). Grounded against the §6 note + propagator.rkt:3404-3467: §6 is a NET-2 `install-typing-network` meta-resolution phenomenon (even a no-op propagator broke it — a multi-meta dependent-notification sensitivity); NET-1 has no meta-resolution dance; A3-narrow installs ZERO NET-2 propagators; the zero-NET-2 invariant under a driven NET-1 holds structurally (the stratum loop reads request cells FROM the net arg + empty-guard skips → provable no-op on the mnr). Tripwire = `first-of-rest` canary; structural guard = δ writes only def-entry cells. §6 root-cause diagnosis → 4C/4D, pain-driven.
- **D2 — `def-bot` + cell-presence discrimination** (no new sentinel, no merge change). Grounded: `def-bot` already exists (definition-entry.rkt:45); today's lookup collapses absent+present-at-bot to #f; the fix is a thin read-side discrimination (cell-id-map presence = known-name; bot-value = pending). `def-entry-merge` untouched. FREE_ORDERING guard: order-insensitive pre-alloc sweep (allocate all names, read nothing to decide). Rejected D2-B (invent a pending-:type sentinel).
- **D3 — narrow-first** (land factory shape; deploy bare-ref slice on the driven mnr — survives reset-meta-store!; general-body = 4C-coupled arc, reset in the SAME arc).
- **D4 — L0 OUT** of the factory scope.
- **D5 — tier dissolves** under pre-allocation (δ installs pre-quiescence; value-tier only if a real mid-fixpoint-discovery case is demonstrated; reject scheduler-coupled 3a).
- **D6 — keep the error-monad shell** for 4B; cell-bottom propagation → future cross-network track (consume the existing def-collision ⊤).

### Q-4B.8 partition LOCKED (§18.21.17)
4B.0 ✅ (decision gate) → **4B.1** dep-recording retire-outright (independent, first; captures part of the 2.5×) → **4B.2** NET-1 becomes driven + def-bot pre-alloc sweep (behavior-preserving substrate) → **4B.3** first-green slice: δ-bare-ref value-defs + the three-way discrimination (active flip; NET-1-only; zero NET-2 propagators; canary-gated; Probe-1b retired here) → **4B.4** widen factory to all env-producing L1/L2 kinds (+ ctor-type, + install-dedup) → **4B.5** general-body/mutual-fn (4C-coupled arc). Deferred (named): Q-4B.7 cell-dependents API (Let-Pain-Drive — zero consumers; would recreate the dead-export); cell-bottom errors; §6 diagnosis. Carried obligation: the LWW single-write SRE obligation (def-entry-merge isn't a join-semilattice → CALM-safety rests on exactly-one value write per def per fixpoint — confirm by-construction at 4B.3/4B.4).

### Methodology data points
- **Two-workflow design-grounding pattern** (grounding-audit → design-options-panel → main-session co-design): kept the main thread lean (file-reading + option-generation off-thread; the main thread held the synthesis + verified facts + co-designed). The NEW `design-options-panel` workflow is the generalization of the audit-delegation pattern to the OPTIONS-proposal stage — propose+adversarial-critique grounded in the process docs, producing co-design MATERIAL not decisions. First production use; validated the "fan out audit + pre-critique, keep Q-resolution in dialogue" discipline (DESIGN_METHODOLOGY § Delegation vs Co-Design).
- **Grounding-before-framing each decision**: D1 + D2 were each grounded against code (the §6 note + the stratum-loop empty-guard for D1; definition-entry.rkt + the lookup adapter for D2) BEFORE framing — so the co-design weighed verified facts, not the panel's assertions. The user couldn't see the agent outputs, so main-session grounding was the trust bridge.
- **The synthesis's first-green-slice insight** (δ-bare-ref on the persistent mnr sidesteps the NET-2 reset) is the move that converts "4B blocked on un-designed 4C" into "deploy narrow now" — the single highest-leverage finding of the panel.

### State + NEXT
- Design-doc only (§18.21.16/.17 + §3 tracker). NO production `.rkt`. Production fire path = champ-diff (`756e1794`); suite 8342/0; branch main; don't push.
- **NEXT: open 4B.1 (dep-recording retire-outright) mini-design + mini-audit** per the Stage 4 Per-Phase Protocol — re-ground the retirement surface at HEAD (the dep-recording identifiers + the dep-edge-hash builder + the 4 test assertions + the save/restore sweep + the bench consumers). First sub-phase; behavior-preserving; cheapest green.

### 4B.2-b SHIPPED (`1ad5add1`, 2026-06-05) — def-bot pre-allocation pass

The pre-alloc half of 4B.2. NET-1 (per-file mnr) now gets a def-bot cell per **user** def/defn name at preparse — the substrate 4B.3's δ residuation propagators reside on.

- **global-env.rkt** — `prealloc-def-cell!` (param-reassigning, sibling of `mnr-add-or-update!`): seeds a def-bot cell IF the name is absent; **idempotent + order-insensitive** (reads only cell-id-map KEY presence, never a cell VALUE → FREE_ORDERING); never clobbers a real value (def-bot is the `def-entry-merge` identity, so we no-op on present). + `(require "definition-entry.rkt")` (pure leaf → no cycle) for def-bot + provide.
- **macros.rkt** — a NEW **Pass 1.5** in `preparse-expand-all` (the choke-point ALL paths flow through → covers process-file / process-string / module-load uniformly) over the **INPUT stxs** (user names only, NOT data/trait-generated — those are 4B.4), 4 heads via `extract-defined-name` + `private-form-base`. A **SEPARATE pass, not a Pass-0 arm** — Pass 0's `with-handlers` would silently swallow a pre-alloc failure (§18.21.19 Q2, scaffolding-hides-truth).

**Behavior-preserving** (verified): a def-bot cell reads `#f` via `def-entry->cons` (= same Unbound as absent); a forward-ref still errors at 4B.2; pre-alloc'd-then-real-write reuses the same cell (LWW); the def-bot cell is transparent to `cascading-lookup` (`(or #f imports)` falls through). +5 tests: prealloc reads #f / order-insensitive / idempotent / no-clobber / **preparse-pass integration** (the last proves Pass 1.5 actually wires prealloc incl. private `def-` — pre-alloc is otherwise invisible to the suite, so a head-match bug would slip).

## PPN 4C Addendum Phase 4B.3-b SHIPPED — the ACTIVE flip (2026-06-06, feat `5a300609`, §18.21.22)

Resumed at 4B.3-b (after a rewind: 4B.3 parent design §18.21.20 LOCKED, 4B.3-a `145c80e7` shipped). The env READ is now cell-at-bot residuation on NET-1 — the §18.20.5 honest-nuance LIFT realized.

### Opener: a FOCUSED grounding-audit (`wf_8539c179-9ed`, 4 facets + critic @ `3637d139`, ~620K tokens)
Scoped to ONLY the net-new wiring (install-on-mnr helper / process-def discrimination+defer+placeholder / lazy-FQN / DQ5 sweep) — NOT re-grounding the already-verified status API / LWW / D1. Every load-bearing claim main-session R-lens-verified. The scoping choice paid off (see ① below).

### ① The load-bearing finding the locked mechanism MISSED — the δ install needs `#:component-paths`
The def-entry input cell is `#:classification 'structural` (phase1d-registrations:300-303) → `enforce-component-paths!` (propagator:1411-1431) **hard-errors** without a declared path (the classification lookup is globally active). The δ unavoidably `:reads` the referent's def-entry cell → MUST pass `#:component-paths (list (cons referent-cid ':value))`. §18.21.20.3's δ spec omitted it; **both the 4B.3 parent grounding AND the locked mechanism missed it** — the focused audit's facet-1 (posed with the wrong "plain cell" premise) refuted the premise. **Wake-semantics RESOLVED BY READING** (no probe, D-4B3-13 cleared): `pu-value-diff` (propagator:1647-1684) returns `#f` ("all changed") for non-hasheq values; a def-entry cell is symbol-when-pending / struct-when-ground (never a hasheq) → the δ wakes unconditionally on `def-bot`→`def-entry`. The `:value` path is the CbC gate-formality (honest: structural-by-classification, whole-value-by-wake).

### ② Dual-write Option A (co-designed against principles)
Real files are under `(ns foo)` → process-def writes BOTH bare `name` (:1150) + `foo::name` (:1156). §18.21.20.3's "referrer-cid" (singular) under-specified it. LOCKED **Option A**: the δ writes the same cell set the normal commit would (parity-exact, both ground together → unambiguous DQ5 sweep). Weighed vs B (FQN-only — leaves a lingering def-bot + an "observationally-safe / never read" red-flag argument): A wins on CbC + parity + no-lingering-state; B's saving is trivial. The user asked for full context (decision/options/tradeoffs/principle-challenge) — the early shorthand (D-1/D-2, DQ3) wasn't legible; re-presented concretely.

### Implementation (feat `5a300609`)
- **namespace.rkt**: `module-network-install-fire-once` — generic install-on-mnr primitive (the δ fire-fn is built driver-side, where `expr-fvar` lives).
- **driver.rkt**: `current-residuation-enabled?` (DQ4 gate, process-file-only — load-module doesn't route through process-file, verified) + `residuation-placeholder` + `try-forward-ref-residuation` (discrimination + lazy-FQN [`qualify-name`] + Option-A δ install) + `finalize-residuations` (DQ5 sweep, status-API by name) + the definition-entry require. The δ `:reads` the BARE referent cell (Pass 1.5-seeded; grounds when `def a` runs) and stores the resolved FQN *name* — only the referrer's `foo::name` needs lazy-pre-alloc.
- **Tracking refinement**: NOT a separate accumulator param (the mini-design sketch) — the placeholder STRUCT sits in the positional `results` list; the sweep amends it in place. Dissolves the cmd-index threading problem.
- Minimal paren-safe process-def edit: a `resid` define + a `[resid resid]` first cond-clause (no far-end surgery).

### Gate ALL GREEN (RF-4, committed-if-green per §18.21.15)
§6 canary `first-of-rest` PASS (the zero-NET-2 sidestep holds under a driven-with-δ NET-1) + structural-guard (test-module-network-01 §2i: δ writes def-entry at cid≥21; install-without-paths throws) + `test-forward-ref-01` NEW (6: forward def, def-chain `y:=x:=a`, backward control, DQ5 file-end shift, immediate typo, the DEF-vs-USE boundary) + acceptance/probe **0 errors** + full suite **8360/0/111.0s** (= 8352 + 8). **Probe-1b RETIRED** (stuck-not-block, never reached residuation). **Carried obligation discharged**: δ = sole value writer per def per fixpoint (defer + fire-once) → LWW exactly-once by construction.

### The DEF-vs-USE boundary (implementation finding, surfaced to user)
Residuation lives in process-def → it residuates DEF BODIES. Forward DEFS (and chains) resolve at the file-end drive. A top-level EXPRESSION *use* of a forward-def BEFORE the drive sees it pending → unbound — the 4C all-at-once / 4B.5 boundary. Sharpens §18.21.20.1 ("resolve without 4C" holds for the residuation SITE = def bodies). NOT a regression (the use errored before too; now the DEF succeeds). The committed test documents it.

### Methodology data points
- **Focused grounding-audit caught what the full parent grounding missed** (the component-paths gap) — validates scoping a follow-on audit to the net-new wiring rather than re-grounding verified surfaces. Strong data point for the focused-audit choice when most surfaces are already grounded.
- **Legibility of co-design options** — the user flagged opaque shorthand (D-1/D-2/DQ3 without grounding); re-presenting each decision as context + options + principle-challenged tradeoffs + lean is the standard. (Watching: keep co-design decisions self-contained.)
- **Implementation-time refinement over the mini-design sketch** (placeholder-struct-in-results vs a tracking param) — a legitimate Stage-4 discovery; persisted to §18.21.22.3.

### State + NEXT
- **HEAD `5a300609`** (feat) + this docs commit on top. Branch `main`, ahead of origin — **don't push**. **Suite baseline 8352 → 8360**. Production fire path still champ-diff. The orthogonal PRELUDE DRIFT warning persists (do NOT `gen-prelude --write`).
- **4B ledger:** 4B.0 ✅ · 4B.1 ✅ `bd8838cf` · 4B.2 ✅ (a/b/c) · **4B.3 ✅ CLOSED** (-a `145c80e7` · -b `5a300609`) · 4B.4 ⬜ NEXT · 4B.5 (general-body, 4C-coupled).
