### The grounding-audit (`wf_17831e43-0e1`, ~705K subagent tokens)
4 read-only HEAD-pinned facets (elaborate-var-seam / discrimination-read / δ-install-and-drive / merge-LWW-and-zeroNET2-guard) + completeness critic @ `80bebf2f`. **All five load-bearing claims main-session R-lens-verified** (the net-add-propagator scheduling branch, the process-def competing-write, Pass 1.5's seed-iff-def-head predicate, the cascade collapse, the elaborate-var branch order — plus the FLAGGED-UNVERIFIED load-module rebuild-discard, now CONFIRMED). The critic surfaced the recurring under-count in 4 places: the FQN-alias double-write, the four-way (not three-way) cell state, the import-cascade local-vs-ground precedence, and the load-module rebuild-discard.

### The single highest-leverage grounded finding
**`net-add-propagator` schedules outside a BSP fire round** (`propagator.rkt:2320-2323`) → the δ installed during per-command Gauss-Seidel is on the mnr worklist → the file-end drive fires it → **bare-ref forward-refs resolve WITHOUT 4C.** This RESOLVES the §18.21.13 "inherently 4B+4C together" worry for the bare-ref slice — the completeness critic closed the Q1/Q3 scheduling question prior probes had left open.

### The 5 design decisions LOCKED (co-designed; options challenged adversarially per principles)
- **DQ1 mechanism = Option A** — δ propagator + process-def DEFERS; the δ is the **deferred commit tail** for the referrer (not a blind copy — A-copy rejected as parity-breaking), sole writer → LWW exactly-once **by construction**. Rejected B (placeholder+δ-overwrite = LWW order-dependence, the CALM unsafety the obligation forbids), C (pure-whnf = Network-Reality-Check fail + type unresolved), D (imperative retry-queue = the killed FREE_ORDERING multi-pass).
- **DQ2 discrimination = factored three-way** `(status . entry|cid|#f)` single cascade-truth source; existing lookups → projections (zero caller change). User leaned **factor-now** for Correct-by-Construction (one cascade-order source, no two-walk drift) over the minimal `pending-cid`.
- **DQ3 FQN = (c) lazy-FQN** (process-def computes the resolved FQN, pre-allocs on-demand) → stored-form parity-exact.
- **DQ4 drive scope = (a) process-file only** — module-load OUT (rebuild-discard → module-loading-on-network, on PM 12 + PM 13/NTT §17b), REPL OUT (no file-end boundary → PPN Track 8/11). Genuine-dependency deferral; the file↔module asymmetry is pre-existing + the common import case works. *(The user pushed back on my one-line DQ4 treatment — re-analyzed at depth with the four options + R-lens'd the rebuild-discard. Methodology data point: a one-line lean isn't enough visibility for an informed choice — same "thin treatment" failure mode flagged at 4A.d/4B.2 Q2.)*
- **DQ5 error-timing = (a) post-drive finalize sweep** — ground → "x : T defined"; still-def-bot → unbound error (forward-ref error shifts immediate→file-end; typos stay immediate, self-correcting via Pass 1.5 seed-iff-def-head).

### The clean realization that fell out of co-design
Working out *where* the discrimination plugs in surfaced a simpler answer than the DQ2 "at elaborate-var" framing: for the bare-ref inferred-path slice, residuation lives at **`process-def`** (a "is body-surf a bare var?" syntactic check + the DQ2 status lookup) — **`elaborate-var` is left UNTOUCHED** (no contract-widening, no nested-residuation risk), and the bare-ref scope is enforced *syntactically* by the body-is-bare-var check (general bodies fall through to status quo = the 4B.5 boundary). Smaller blast radius than threading a signal through elaborate-var.

### Partition (§18.21.20.5)
**4B.3-a** (DQ2 factoring — three-way status lookup + existing lookups as projections; behavior-preserving) → **4B.3-b** (the active flip: process-def discrimination + DQ3 lazy-FQN + δ fire-once + defer + DQ5 sweep; RF-4-gated; atomic since residuation + finalization are one correctness unit; may sub-split at its open).

### Methodology data points
- **grounding-audit workflow ~7th production use** — kept the main thread lean (~705K off-thread); the completeness critic caught the FQN double-write + four-way-state + rebuild-discard under-counts the facets/design framing missed; main-session R-lens-verified the 5 load-bearing claims surgically (targeted reads, not full re-reads).
- **design-options-panel NOT used** (unlike 4B.0) — the seam was focused (one foundational DQ + dependents), so main-session co-design dialogue was right-sized; the panel is for wide/uncertain option spaces.
- **"factor now for CbC" + the process-def-not-elaborate-var simplification** both emerged from the co-design dialogue, not the grounding — the grounding gave the facts; the architecture fell out of weighing them against principles with the user.
- **Thin-treatment pushback (DQ4)** — recurring: a one-line lean doesn't give the user visibility to make an informed choice; depth + R-lens were required. Reinforces the per-decision adversarial-options discipline.

### State + NEXT
- Design-doc (§18.21.20) + §3 tracker row 4B + this dailies persisted. Production `.rkt` UNCHANGED (`31925625`); suite 8349/0; branch main; don't push.
- **NEXT: implement 4B.3-a** (the DQ2 factoring — three-way status lookup + reimplement `global-env-lookup-type/value` + `module-network-cascading-lookup` as projections; behavior-preserving). Open its mini-design + mini-audit per Stage 4 (re-ground the lookup surfaces + the ~30 caller blast radius); gate = suite green + projection-agreement + cascade-order-agreement tests. Then 4B.3-b (the active flip, RF-4-gated).

## PPN 4C Addendum Phase 4B.3-a SHIPPED — DQ2 three-way status factoring (2026-06-05, feat `145c80e7`, §18.21.21)

Opened 4B.3-a per the Stage 4 Per-Phase Protocol (mini-design + targeted main-session mini-audit — NOT the grounding-audit workflow; the surface was line-cited at §18.21.20, well-scoped behavior-preserving factoring). Implemented, gated GREEN, committed.

### Mini-audit caught a grounding under-count
The 4B.3 grounding-audit reported `module-network-cascading-lookup` has "exactly two production callers" (global-env x2). The 4B.3-a re-grep found a **THIRD: `lsp/server.rkt:404`** (`(and mnr (module-network-cascading-lookup mnr sym))`). The recurring "enumeration under-counts" failure mode — this time in the *grounding facet's own* enumeration. Reimplementing cascading-lookup as a projection preserves the `(cons type value)|#f` contract → lsp/server safe (gated by `test-lsp-repl-01`, green). Reinforces: the per-sub-phase mini-audit re-greps even when the parent grounding "covered" the surface.

### The factoring (behavior-exact, perf-neutral)
- **namespace.rkt**: new `module-network-lookup-status mnr name → (cons status payload)` — the SINGLE cascade-truth source, walks local-ground → import-ground → local-pending → absent (`'ground . (type . value)` / `'pending . cid` / `'absent . #f`). `module-network-cascading-lookup` reimplemented as its ground-projection. Reuses `def-entry->cons` for the local read → the def-bot→#f collapse AND the def-collision loud-error path are parity-exact; the import sub-walk reuses the cascading-lookup projection (mutually recursive, acyclic-DAG-terminating) → import-shadowing preserved (local def-bot → import-ground fires before 'pending).
- **global-env.rkt**: new `global-env-lookup-status` (param-keyed; the 4B.3-b consumer API; #f mnr → 'absent); `global-env-lookup-type/value` reimplemented as projections of it.
- The `pending`/`absent` split is **computed but UNCONSUMED** until 4B.3-b (both project → #f). Same hash-ref + import-walk + per-call-site walk count → behavior-preserving AND perf-neutral (one O(1) tag added after the walk).

### Gate GREEN
check-parens 3 files; `raco make driver` clean (23s); targeted **119/119** (test-module-network-01 +3, test-foreign + test-lsp-repl-01 = the cascading-lookup consumers); acceptance `2026-04-17-ppn-track4c.prologos` **0 errors** + probe clean (process-file Level 3); full suite **8352 all-pass** (= 8349 + 3). The lone batch-fail `test-facet-sre-registration` is the KNOWN SRE-registration linklet-staleness (passes **21/21 isolated**; orthogonal to 4B.3-a — my change doesn't touch facet-sre). VAG (2-column adversarial) clean: behavior-preserving + perf-neutral verified; `pending`-unconsumed is honest substrate-staging, NOT validated≠deployed (not flag-gated; the partition explicitly stages it for 4B.3-b).

### 3 tests (test-module-network-01, 55→58)
status-shape (`('ground . (t.v))` / `('pending . cid)` / `('absent . #f)`) + projection-agreement (cascading-lookup/type/value/status return the pre-factoring values; +no-mnr→'absent) + cascade-order-agreement (the two-walk-drift guard, D-4B3-1: def-bot-local-but-ground-in-import → resolves to the IMPORT not 'pending; local-ground shadows import; def-bot-local + absent-import → 'pending).

### Methodology data point
- **Per-sub-phase mini-audit re-grep is load-bearing even after a parent grounding** — the grounding's own enumeration under-counted (missed lsp/server.rkt:404). The targeted re-grep at sub-phase open is the catch; another data point for "audit-first / enumeration-under-counts" (the grounding ≠ exhaustive; the sub-phase audit grounds its own surface).
